  - A qualifier that contradicts its year is `INVALID_MONTH`.
- **Month end.** Closing quantities is refused with 422 `MONTH_NOT_ENDED` (`details.monthEndsAt`) until the month is over. The month is over at the later of the plant-local midnight after its last date and the end of its last shift or period. This applies whatever the state of its periods, and to SuperAdmin too.
- **Expected periods.** The prerequisites also count every scheduled shift of the month that has no production period yet. A shift counts when it is not excluded, its unit has an approved profile at the shift start, and no period overlaps it (the materializer's test). Each one blocks the close (`CLOSE_PREREQUISITES`, with `missingPeriodCount` / `missingPeriods`).
- **When a month closes.** All of these must hold:
  - it has ended;
  - every expected period exists and is approved;
  - every day is certified (policy);
  - no change proposal is open (policy).
- The view adds `prerequisites.missingPeriodCount`, `missingPeriods`, `monthEnded` and `monthEndsAt`, and `met` includes them. Month close reads "now" from the injected `Clock`. No calculation changed.
- Tests: `src/domain/close/month-key.spec.ts`, `test/integration/month-close.int-spec.ts`.

## 2026-10-18 — Marun regression follow-ups (`docs/MARUN_E2E_REPORT.md` §4: R1–R3, G1–G4; engineering default)

- **R1. The coding state follows the event's data on every write.** `prepareUpdate` (every v1 / v2 / bulk / source / merge write without an explicit legacy status) recomputes it from the merged reason: a reason → `coded` (a `reviewed` coding stays reviewed); a stop without a reason → `uncoded`, unless its investigation is pending (then the stored state stands, as before). A non-stop event without a reason keeps its stored state unless the write clears its reason (v1 workflows close advisory events without a reason). An explicit legacy `status` (v1 PUT) still decides, as before. So an OEE-only edit of a reasonless "Coded" stop now uncodes it.
  - Whenever a write moves the coding state to `uncoded`, the supervisor verification (`verified_by` / `verified_at`) is cleared too (`uncodedVerificationReset`, user and source writes), whatever field changed. Maintenance validation is left alone (it is about the failure detail).
  - Migration `1794221600000-UncodeReasonlessCodedStops` (data repair, idempotent): downtime events with `reason_code_id IS NULL`, not `investigation_pending` and `coding_state = 'coded'` become `uncoded`, verification cleared, legacy status "Coded" → "Open" (an RCA or retraction label stays), `row_version` + 1. No event revision is written (repair of a derived state; the coding history is unchanged). `down` is a no-op (documented in the class).
- **R2. The day coding line counts and rates the same shifts.** The day gate's coded ratio now sums the period `coded_downtime_ratio` of the current run of every shift's latest non-cancelled revision (approved or not), the same shifts whose stops it counts. Once every shift is approved — the only time a day can be certified — this is the approved runs, as before, so no certification verdict changes. A shift without a calculated run is still missing from the ratio while its stops count (rare: a period that was never calculated).
- **R3. A proposal names a changed cause classification.** Each `after.codingChanges` row adds `fields` (`oeeComponent`, `scheduleClass`, `impactMode`; before / after) when the cause event's classification at the approved run's event revision (`loss_line_evidence.event_revision_id` → `event_revisions.effective_facts`) differs from the new inputs; a row is listed when the reason or any of these changed. Additive inside the proposal snapshot (no OpenAPI schema for it). The panel reads e.g. "OEE component changed: Performance → Availability · availability loss line 14.00". The impact code is not named (impact = the impact mode).
- **G1. Month-close overview** shows, next to "Close quantities…", "Month ends <date> — not ended yet" (or "Month ended …") from `prerequisites.monthEnded` / `monthEndsAt`, and the count of expected periods that do not exist yet with the first ten of `missingPeriods`.
- **G2. My RCA actions.** "All RCA actions" opens `/rca?tab=actions&mine=true&status=all` (every action you own, any status). The owner screen (a member without analysis roles) has a status filter `?status=open|done|all` (default: open or in progress); the list is always the user's own and has no self-link.
- **G3.** The PDF cover prints "Selection: N named approved revision(s)" for a revision-id export instead of a count under "Window (plant time)". Loss-line basis keys are printed in words (PDF: "Planning rate"; UI: `lossBasisLabel`), and the actuals provenance names a segment method (`counter_delta/step:delta` → "counter difference, step interpolation, counted"; `segmentMethodLabel`, unknown parts humanized). Maps live in `src/features/ux-kit/labels/accounting-labels.ts`.
- **G4. Closed investigations take no new link.** `POST /v2/rcas/{id}/links` and `POST /v2/production-events/{id}/rca-link` answer 409 `CASE_CLOSED` for a new link to a `Closed` case (a repeat of an existing link stays 200); reopen the case (independent approver) first. This tightens the contract (earlier a late link was accepted and set the event's RCA state to `completed`); the OpenAPI descriptions and `schema.d.ts` are regenerated. The Links tab of a closed case shows a note instead of the add-link form. The loss-line picker shows "Linked" also for a line linked under an earlier run's id of the same period (same period, kind, cause event and segment, read from the link's stored evidence).
- **Recommendations of cases closed before the closure rule.** Migration `1794221700000-CloseRecommendationsOfClosedCases` applies the rule of "Marun E2E RCA follow-up fixes" once to the recommendations of every `Closed` case (all implementing actions done, cancelled aside → Completed; all cancelled → Cancelled; none or already final → unchanged). The rule is copied into the migration so the repair keeps its meaning; no audit row is written; `row_version` + 1. `down` is a no-op (documented).
- **Impact basis names the asset with its code:** "From the approved impact rule for Main drive and gearbox (EXT1-Main)".
- No calculation changed. Tests: `src/production-event/event-write-model.spec.ts`, `src/domain/events/coding-verification.spec.ts`, `src/reconciliation/proposal-trigger.spec.ts`, `src/exports/pdf/export-pdf.spec.ts`; integration `stop-events`, `day-certification`, `reconciliation-late-corrections`, `rca-cases`; frontend `proposal-labels`, `month-close-sections`, `rca-u3-flows`, `rca-u3-links`, `labels`, `impact-basis`.


## 2026-10-18 — Owner: "do according to standards" (open questions closed)

- **Approved revision Value section:** shows the valuation view frozen at approval (approved records are immutable). When the current policy's default differs, a "Restatement pending under policy vN" note links to the change proposal; the current view is offered as a secondary toggle, never silently substituted.
- **RCA effectiveness coverage:** new RCA policy field `effectiveness.minimumCoverage` (default 0.95). A post-action window below it → `insufficient_evidence`; coverage always shown.
- **Units without a state mapping:** verdict stays `insufficient_evidence` with the reason "needs a state mapping for this unit".
- **Named verifier:** only the named verifier (or SuperAdmin) may mark an action done (maker-checker).
- **15:31Z Shift C edit:** owner manual test (owner tested manually on 18 Oct).

## 2026-10-18 — Owner "do according to standards": implementation (engineering default)

Implements "Owner: 'do according to standards' (open questions closed)".
- **Approved revision Value section.** `GET /v2/production-periods/{id}/reconciliation` adds `approvedValuation` (additive; null unless the shown revision is approved and its approval snapshot froze a valuation): the snapshot's default view and policy id / version (`content.reviewed.valuation`), the current approved tenant-wide policy's default view and version (same rule as the report default, SME V8), `differs`, and `restatementProposalId` (the open change proposal of the published revision, else null). The Value section asks `GET /reports/loss-value` for the frozen view by name, so the approved figures stay the ones stored at approval. When the views differ it shows "Restatement pending under valuation policy vN" with a link to the period's History & exports (the proposal) or "No restatement opened", and a secondary toggle "Current policy view (not approved)"; while toggled, the status badge reads "Current policy view (not approved)", never "Approved". Draft and in-review revisions show the current policy view as before. No figure is recomputed.
- **RCA minimum coverage.** `rca_policy.effectiveness.minimumCoverage` (decimal 0–1, default 0.95; parser 422 `INVALID_POLICY` outside [0, 1]; schema and seed default updated; DTO description updated, OpenAPI snapshot refreshed). A policy version without the field behaves as 0.95, and so does an action baseline whose criteria were frozen before the field (the criteria freeze `minimumCoverage` from now on). This replaces the "complete coverage" default of "Marun E2E RCA follow-up fixes". Post-action coverage below the minimum → `insufficient_evidence` with "state data covers X % of the window after the action (H h without data); a verdict needs at least Y % coverage"; at or above → the verdict comes from the known exposure. Each new verdict stores `coverage` (`baseline`, `post`, `minimum`); the baseline window is reported, not gated. Verdicts already stored are not re-evaluated.
- **Units without a state mapping.** The exposure window lists `unmappedUnitIds` (case units with no state-mapping version overlapping the window); such a post window verifies as `insufficient_evidence` with "needs a state mapping for this unit" (several: "needs a state mapping for each of these N units") ahead of the coverage check.
- **Named verifier.** Already enforced (`action-completion.ts`, 403 `NOT_THE_VERIFIER`, SuperAdmin excepted) and covered by `test/integration/rca-action-owners.int-spec.ts`; a unit test of the rule with the SuperAdmin exception is added (`super-admin-independence.spec.ts`).


## 2026-10-18 — Shared work-item notifications, read / unread, approval detail page (owner findings; engineering default to standard practice)

- **Notifications are per person, the work is shared.** Every notification that asks for work carries `work_item_key` (record type + id + the work owed; level and recipient left out): `uncoded:<unit>:<day>`, `long-stop:<event>`, `rca-action:<id>:<due>`, `task:<id>:<due>`, `sla:<period>:due:<stage>:<revision>:<rejections>`, `sla:<period>:approval` (age escalations), `sla:<period>:rejection:<revision>`. Source fallbacks are information only (null key, never resolved). Key rules: `src/domain/notifications/work-item-key.ts`; the producers set it in `insertNotifications`.
- **Resolution rule (per work item, for every recipient at once):** uncoded backlog — the unit and production day has no closed uncoded unplanned stop left (coded, marked for investigation, reclassified or retracted); long open stop — the stop ended, was reclassified or is gone; RCA action / task — no longer open for the reminded due date (`completed` when done / cancelled / deleted, `superseded` when open under a new due date); period SLA — the period's current revision is approved, or (due reminder) its stage / submission cycle is no longer current, or (rejection escalation) its revision is no longer current. Resolved rows keep their history (`resolved_at`, `resolution`), leave the active list and the unread count, and are listed with `GET /v2/notifications?state=resolved`.
- **Trigger:** set-based SQL per plant (one UPDATE per rule over open rows, `IDX_user_notifications_open_work`; no N+1) run (a) after the domain messages that can finish work — `EventChanged` / `EventRetracted` (not verification-only) and the period workflow messages (submitted, approved, rejected, revision opened / cancelled, stale) — coalesced per plant for 1 s so a burst resolves once and the outbox relay is never held up (`NotificationResolutionModule`, imported by the API root for the `all` role and the worker), and (b) by a one-minute sweep of plants with open work items, which also covers work changed without a message (RCA actions, tasks, due dates). A resolution publishes `UserNotificationsResolved` (outbox, aggregate `user_notification`, no content) so every bell of the plant refetches.
- **Work owed again re-opens** the same notification (same user and dedupe key) as new and unread (e.g. a stop's reason is cleared at the same reminder level); a superseded due date never comes back unless the date does.
- **Read / unread:** per recipient (`read_at`). New `POST /v2/notifications/{id}/unread`. The bell has All / Unread filters (server `unread=true`), an "Unread" text label and a left border on unread items (not colour only), per-item "Mark as read" / "Mark as unread", "Mark all read", and the unread badge (active notifications only).
- **API (additive):** `GET /v2/notifications` adds `state=active|resolved|all` (default `active` — resolved shared work is no longer in the default list, which is the requested behaviour change) and `workItemKey`, `resolvedAt`, `resolution` on each item; `unread-count` counts active unread only. `GET /v2/approval-inbox/{kind}/{id}`: one pending record with the list's role / scope filter and separation-of-duties actions, 404 once decided or outside the caller's approval scope, 400 for an unknown kind. Migration `1794222350000-NotificationWorkItemResolution` (columns, check, backfill of keys for existing rows, active / unread / open-work partial indexes; `down` restores the previous unread index and drops the columns).
- **Approval detail page `/approvals/:kind/:id`** for every inbox kind: what needs approval (workflow, record and version, state, unit, requested by, submitted at, approval roles, summary), the record content as it is now (the existing record read, incl. effective dates and evidence fields), "Your decision" (the decisions open to the user, blocked ones with the server's separation-of-duties reason, SuperAdmin note), and the actions the workflow supports through the existing commands (approve / reject / return with reason / retire / activate; retire only where the lifecycle has it; no new business rule). After a confirmed decision the page toasts and returns to the inbox, where the record is already removed (2026-10-17 rule). "Open full workspace" stays as the secondary action. Kinds without an inbox command (period review, restatement posting, change proposal, RCA closure, month close steps, duplicate-stop review, source status rules) show their summary and open their review workspace as the primary action, since their evidence and gates live there. A record decided elsewhere shows "No longer pending" with the way back. Inbox rows link to the page ("Review" and the title); the "Review details" dialog is replaced by the page. The dashboard "Pending approvals" tile opens the single record when exactly one waits, else the inbox.
- **Open:** a before / after comparison against the approved predecessor is not generic across the 25 kinds; the page shows the draft as it is now, and the workspace keeps version history. No approval-request notifications exist yet (approvals are pulled from the inbox); SLA period reminders are the approval notifications that resolve on decision.


## 2026-10-18 — Owner findings: actor names, one status wording, Integration for SuperAdmin only (engineering default to standard practice)

- **Who did it (finding 1).** One way to show a person: `useUserNames` / `<PersonName id>` / `<ActorName actor>` and the pure `actorText` / `resolvedActorText` (`src/features/ux-kit/labels`). Plant members come from the cached member directory (one call); any other id — passed in or first met while rendering — is looked up by id, batched into one `GET /v2/users/directory?ids=` call per render pass (≤ 100 ids per call) and cached per tenant and id (no N+1, TTL = the users stale time). Wording (audit-trail practice: attributable, never anonymous): the person's name; **"Former user"** when the plant directory no longer returns the id (deactivated elsewhere, another plant); **"Unknown user"** only when the directory itself was refused; **"System"** for automatic actors, with the source when known — "System (state engine)", "System (accounting recalculation)", "System (<connector source>)" for connector revisions. Never "a user", "Someone" or an id. Server-resolved names (`actorName`, `createdByName`, month-close `PersonRef`, RCA history) are used as sent. The event "Values" table says "Changed by <name>" (the user revision that last changed the overridden field) instead of "Changed by a user"; the coding history says "classification set manually · by <name>". The backend audit CSV keeps "Unknown user" (export contract unchanged; open for the owner).
- **Status wording (finding 2).** The coding state of a stop and the accounting workflow of its periods are different dimensions (ISA-95 event vs. accounting record) and are shown side by side, never one instead of the other. Event reads (list and single) add `accountingState` (additive, optional; `none` / `draft` / `in_review` / `stale` / `approved` — the period grid's `workflowState` of the overlapping periods, one query per page; when they differ, open work wins: in review, then inputs changed, then draft, approved only when every calculated period is approved). The Event Hub list and the event summary show the coding chip ("Uncoded" / "Coded") plus "Accounting: in review" (nothing before any period is calculated). Every role reads the same server field. The period workflow has one label map (`GRID_WORKFLOW_LABELS`) and one tone map (`GRID_WORKFLOW_TONES`; `REVISION_LIFECYCLE_TONES` for revisions) used by the period grid, PLA, month close ("Waiting for approval" / "Not reconciled" removed) and the event's impacted periods, which now show the server's workflow state instead of the display-only readiness. A submitted revision whose inputs changed reads "In review (inputs changed)" (it is still in review), amber.
- **Integration (finding 3).** The Integration section (Connectivity, Source Status Rules, Source Priority, Enhanced Historian) is listed and routed for SuperAdmin only (`canReachIntegration`), with a route gate and a page gate (`IntegrationPageGate`). Least privilege with maker-checker kept: the Source Priority page stays reachable (not listed) for its approvers (production accounting, `canReviewSourcePriority`) and the Source Status Rules page for the source owner (`canReviewSourceStatusRules`), who open them from the approvals inbox / approval detail page; Source Priority can also be decided in the inbox itself. Server role rules are unchanged (the API stays authoritative; integration administrators keep their API rights, e.g. for automation). The Equipment Setup Guide's connection steps follow the same gate; the master-data "Data source mappings" note links to the console only for SuperAdmin.


## 2026-10-18 — "Some values were not accepted" (owner manual test): refusals name the field (engineering default)

- **What the owner saw.** Title + "Check the highlighted values and try again." with nothing highlighted and no field named. The backend logs / live DB were not reachable for this check (Docker stopped), so the cause was found statically: (a) the error kit replaced the server's own reason with that generic text for every `BAD_REQUEST` / `VALIDATION_FAILED` / unmapped 400/422, even when no field was refused; (b) some screens dropped the field list — the finance refusal alert (valuation policy, FX, demand status), the master-data record editor (no toast when a field was refused, and "Check this value." on the input), the failure-dictionary forms (message only), the event form when every refused field had an input; (c) a property the screen does not render (a whitelist refusal "property x should not exist") was shown nowhere.
- **Rule.** Every refusal names what was refused. The alert / toast lists each refused field as "Label: reason" (also when the input is highlighted); a property the server does not accept at all reads **"Unexpected field: xyz"** and is never put on an input (it is the screen's fault: "This screen sent a field the server does not accept. Report the field named below with the support reference."). A generic refusal without field errors shows the server's sentence; when the server gave none, "The server did not say which value. Copy the support reference and include it when you report this." — never "highlighted" when nothing is.
- **Contract guard.** `src/api/v2/payload-contract.test.ts` checks the main command builders (event create / edit, asset, unit, product, RCA template, valuation policy, FX rate, RCA action and cause, tasks, plan create / version edit, actuals adjustment, lot disposition) against `server/pro-act-backend/openapi/v2.json` (undeclared properties at any depth, missing required). Static sweep result (TypeScript checker over every `v2.*` / `v2Request` command and GET query, plus `npm run api:audit -- --static-only` against the DTO whitelists): no payload sends an undeclared property and the OpenAPI request schemas equal the class-validator whitelists for all 187 v2 body routes.
- **Read-model echo fixed.** A template save sent the stored logic tree back as read (`template_data` is returned raw, so v1 / imported nodes can carry keys `LogicTreeNodeDto` does not declare); the payload builder now sends only the DTO's node fields (`logicTreeBodyOf`).
- **Open (owner).** If the backend container predates today's additive DTO fields while the dev frontend is current, these sends are refused until the backend is rebuilt: event edit `changeReason`; valuation policy `backdateReason`, `restateApproved`; source authority / micro-stop / stop-event policy `effectiveFrom`, `backdateReason`; RCA action `verificationMethod`, `verifierId`, `recommendationId`, `cancelReason`; task `rcaActionId`; `POST /v2/rcas/{id}/causes`, `PATCH /v2/rca-case-causes/{id}`. With this change such a refusal reads "Unexpected field: <name>".


## 2026-10-19 — Owner manual-test gaps (`docs/MARUN_E2E_REPORT.md` §5: finding 1 partial, O1, O2, restatement postings, field labels, header revision; engineering default to standard practice)

- **Automatic actors are attributable (finding 1).** `GET /v2/production-events/{id}/revisions` adds `actorSource` on each revision and `authorSource` on each coding revision (additive; null for people). The writers store only `actor_kind`, so the source is derived server-side from what the revision records (`revision-actor-source.ts`): the writer's own change reason ("state engine…" → `state-engine`, "unit impact…" → `unit-impact`, "Repair:…" → `data-repair`, duplicate merge → `stop-reconciliation`, split / merge → `event-operations`), else a state-engine-derived event's source / retraction / baseline coding → `state-engine`, a connector or import revision → the event's source name. The History tab reads "System (state engine)", "System (data repair)", "System (<source name>)"; "System" alone only when nothing recorded names the service. No schema change (storing the service per revision is the follow-up if more automatic writers appear).
- **Current state vs. history (O1).** Revision snapshots are append-only history; current-state displays read the event row. The event Values tab's "In use" column takes status, coding / physical / RCA state, reason, impact code and classification from the event read, with the note "the History tab lists the values recorded at each revision (latest: revision N)". The R1 repair, which changed the row without a revision, is recorded by migration `1794222400000-RecordUncodedStopRepairRevision`: one `system` revision per repaired event (actor System, reason "Repair: reasonless stop marked uncoded", snapshot = the latest one with the row's coding state and status); idempotent; `down` removes exactly these while still latest. On the live plant this appends revision 5 to the Shift C stop `3ea50fb2…`; history rows are not edited.
- **Open work-item notifications state the work as it is now (O2).** After each resolution pass (domain message or one-minute sweep) every open `uncoded_backlog` notification whose unit-day is still owed gets the current `stops`, `uncodedSeconds`, `oldestEndedAt` and message (one set-based UPDATE, only changed rows, all recipients at once); read state, level and alert time are kept, and a refresh also makes every bell of the plant refetch (`UserNotificationsResolved` payload gains `refreshed`). One notification per work item, recipient and level remains; lists stay newest first by alert time (unchanged). The live 17 Oct items read "1 stop(s)" after the first sweep following the deploy; no data migration needed.
- **Restatement postings (finding 4) — no product bug.** Live DB: Kamran (`kamran@hdpe-train.mpc`, legacy role Analyst) holds `finance_approver` at tenant scope in HDPE-TRAIN, as the seeder grants. The inbox kind `finance-posting` (roles `finance_approver`, `approvalScope` → TRUE for a tenant grant), `POST …/revisions/:n/posting` (`POSTING_DECIDERS`) and the frontend `canDecidePosting` all accept him; the five items are authored by Reza, so separation of duties does not block him. The verification checked only SuperAdmin, Reza and Maryam. Locked by unit tests (backend `approval-access.spec.ts`, frontend `capabilities.test.ts`). No data repair.
- **Refusals use the form's words.** `describeError(error, context, fields)` and `serverFieldErrors(error, fields)` take a per-form `FieldVocabulary` (server path or root → `{ label, values }`): the field reads with the form's label, enum lists ("must be one of the following values: …") with the form's choice labels, a field name inside the reason with its label. Without a vocabulary the path and the choices are humanized (never raw keys). `FinanceRefusalAlert`, `AdminRefusalAlert`, `FriendlyErrorAlert` and `friendlyToastContent` accept it; the valuation policy form passes `POLICY_FIELD_VOCABULARY` ("Default management view", "Time-based costs", …).
- **Event header revision.** The header reads "<origin> · revision N" with N = the newest revision in the history (as the History tab lists), not `rowVersion`; without the history loaded only the origin is shown.


## 2026-10-19 — Hierarchy names (owner)

Organization renamed "Marun" (was "Marun learning organization") and plant "HDPE plant" (was "HDPE training plant"); codes MPC-TRAIN / HDPE-TRAIN unchanged. Done through the application as SuperAdmin; the setup seeder (`scripts/marun-train/train-catalog.ts`) uses the new names.


## 2026-10-19 — Approvals: decide in place, exactly what changes, exact deep links (owner: "we have to go in depth just to add or approve anything"; engineering default to standard approval-inbox practice)

- **Decide in place for every inbox kind.** The approval page `/approvals/:kind/:id` renders the decision of every kind with the workflow's existing command, gates and confirmations (no new business rule; the server stays authoritative): the inbox commands as before (versions, plans, policies, source priority, adjustments, dispositions, budgets, rules); **period review** — the reconciliation equation (`ReconciliationSummary`), the bridge review where the policy asks for it, approve (summary + optional comment) / reject (reason + failed lines) with the workspace dialogs and `approveGate` / `reviewGate`; **restatement posting** — operational date + the workspace posting date / treatment form (finance approvers); **change proposal** — the proposal card with accept / dismiss and a reason (not the proposer); **RCA closure** — the case workflow panel (server readiness, independent approver); **month close** — only the step the record waits for (materiality review, finance sign-off, lock with the typed month); **duplicate stop** — merge / keep both with a reason; **source status rules** — the version card's approve. After a server-confirmed decision the record leaves every cached inbox page, a toast names the decision and the page returns to the inbox (2026-10-17 rule). Forms that do not report success themselves are watched: the decision counts once the refreshed record, seen pending on the page, is no longer pending. "Open in workspace" stays as the secondary action.
- **Who may decide (list and page).** Inbox rows add `canDecide` and `decisionBlockedReason` (additive): for kinds with inbox commands, any open command (retire included); for the other kinds, the requester rule of that step (submitter, proposer of a manual proposal — now the row's author —, closure requester, quantity closer) with SuperAdmin exempt; restatement posting and month lock carry no requester rule (as in the server). Finer content rules (line authors, the reopener, bridge reviewer, investigation independence, materiality reviewer ≠ finance signer) are shown by the inline form from the workspace gates / server readiness and enforced by the command. **"Only what I can decide"** (`GET /v2/approval-inbox?decidable=true`) filters in SQL with the same rule, so keyset pages stay complete.
- **Exactly the value needing approval.** New `GET /v2/approval-inbox/{kind}/{id}/changes` (same role / scope / 404 / 400 as the record read) returns `{ kind, id, mode, beforeLabel, afterLabel, fields: [{ key, label, before, after, unit, currency, format, group, changed }] }`. Predecessor per kind: versioned master data — the latest approved version of the same family (the key `approveVersion` locks) starting at or before the draft; plan — `supersedesVersionId`, else the approved version of the plan (target, Σ manual slice targets, slices, window, basis, distribution); valuation policy / source priority — `basedOnId`, else the approved policy of the same kind and scope (body as labelled leaf paths); source status rules — the approved rules of the source (per status); disposition — the disposition it supersedes; uncertainty budget — the approved budget of the unit and boundary; rule — the active version's definition; period review / restatement posting — the reviewed figures of the revision against the approved revision it supersedes (`figuresOf` + `restatementDelta`: target, actual, attributed, Unaccounted, gap, tolerance adjustment per unit, actuals, changed loss lines by reason); change proposal — approved vs new-input slices, the approved loss value per currency (display currency shown) and the cause-coding changes. No predecessor (`mode: full`): the full pending content (adjustments, a first version, RCA closure, month close steps). Duplicate stop (`mode: compare`): the suggested duplicate next to the stop kept. Referenced ids are replaced by names server-side (one query per table per response); values are sent as decimal strings / ISO instants and formatted on the client with the ux-kit (`Quantity`, `PlantTime`, plant digits, money in its currency, display currency for multi-currency figures). Unchanged fields are folded behind "Show unchanged fields (n)".
- **Inbox rows.** Grouped by workflow (groups of the current page, oldest first); each row shows the one-line change (`headline`, additive: first changed field, or the leading value without predecessor — "Rate 28 → 29 t/h") and the primary action "Review & decide" (or "View" with the reason). Headlines are computed per page in one batch per kind; period review and restatement posting rows keep their summary (their figures need two full revision loads — read on the approval page only, never per list row).
- **Exact deep links.** `reviewHref` now names the exact place (value change, same field): `/planning/profiles/:id#profile-version`, `…#rate-<id>`, `…#valuation-rate-<id>`, `/planning?tab=transitions#transition-standard-<id>`, `/planning?tab=streams#unit-stream-<id>`, `/master-data/plant-flow#flow-route-<id>`, `/master-data/unit-impact-rules#unit-impact-rule-<id>`, `/planning/plans/:planId#plan-version-<n>`, `/finance?tab=demand#demand-status-<id>`, `/connectivity/source-authority?unitId=…#source-authority-version-<id>`, `/connectivity/source-status#status-mapping-<id>`, `/plans/:plan/periods/:period/actuals#adjustment-<id>`, the period of a disposition's production start `…/actuals#disposition-<id>`, `/measurement-uncertainty?unitId=…#uncertainty-budget-<id>`, `/plans/:plan/periods/:period/review#period-review`, `…/review#restatement`, `…/history#change-proposal-<id>`, `/rca/:id#rca-workflow` (fixes the broken `/rca/cases/:id`), `/month-close?month=…#close-step-<step>`, `/events/:kept?tab=related#stop-merge-<id>`; fx, policy and rule links open their record page. Rows add `target` (`periodId`, `planId`, `revision`, `monthKey`, `eventId`, `profileId`) for the inline forms. A shared `useHighlightTarget()` (mounted once in the app shell) waits for the element with the hash id, scrolls it into view, focuses it, rings it for 2.5 s and announces "Showing …" in a polite live region (or that the item is not on the page any more); `DataTable` gains `getRowAnchor` for row ids.
- **Not inbox kinds (unchanged).** Bridge review is decided inside the period review form (its panel is shown there), not as its own inbox row; data holds are a period writer's pause without an approver or separation of duties, so they stay out of the inbox (adding them would be a new rule).
- **Open (owner).** The server accepts a duplicate-stop decision from any event writer while the inbox lists it for manager approvers / production engineers; restatement posting has no requester rule on the server (`ownMayDecide`) — confirm both are intended. A rate deep link to a version on an older page of the rate table is announced as not on the page.


## 2026-10-19 — Approval follow-ups and hierarchy editing (owner: "go with suggestion")

- **Duplicate-stop decisions:** the approval inbox shows them to exactly the roles the server accepts for the decision (event writers), so who sees the item matches who can decide it.
- **Restatement posting independence:** the finance approver who sets a posting date must not be the person who proposed the correction (manual change proposal author) — new server check (403 SEPARATION_OF_DUTIES), SuperAdmin exception as for other self-decisions.
- **Deep links to items on later pages:** the link carries the item id; the target table resolves the page that contains it and opens it, then highlights the row.
- **Organization / plant editing (Admin panel):** name and description editable (SuperAdmin; OrgAdmin within their own organization); codes are immutable after creation (referenced by history, exports and integrations); the plant time zone is editable only while the plant has no events, samples or calculated periods — otherwise read-only with the reason. Optimistic concurrency (row version), audited, plant-access rules unchanged.
  - *Implementation (standard master-data practice):* `PATCH /v2/organizations/:id` and `PATCH /v2/tenants/:id` with `If-Match` (428 without, 412 STALE_REVISION when stale; new `row_version` on `organizations` / `tenants`, migration `HierarchyRowVersion`). Code and organization are not accepted in the body (422 VALIDATION_FAILED on v2, by the whitelist). A time zone change on a plant with data answers 409 TIMEZONE_LOCKED with capped counts (events, signal samples, periods with a calculation run or accounting revision); `GET /v2/tenants/:id` exposes `timezoneEditable` / `timezoneLockedReason` to editors (field casing follows the existing `timezone`). The lock check and the site sync run outside the caller's row-level-security binding (after the organization check), so an OrgAdmin without a scope in the plant cannot unlock it by not seeing its data.
  - *Sites:* today a plant has exactly one default site (named after it, carrying the zone displays and new calendars use), so sites are not edited separately: a plant time zone change moves the default site's zone (and clears its UTC fallback flag); a rename renames the default site while it still carries the old plant name. Published calendar versions keep the zone they were published with. Units and assets stay in Master data (the plant edit page links there).
  - *UI:* Edit actions in Admin panel → Plants & organizations and on organization / plant nodes of Master data → Plant hierarchy (server `canEdit` flag); on 412 the page loads the latest version and keeps the user's text; the header plant selection takes the new name at once.


## 2026-10-19 — QA audit decisions (owner; docs/qa/QA_SUMMARY.md)

- **Roles = guide roles only:** each persona holds exactly the plant role the guide assigns; the legacy-role mirror must not add production_engineer to approvers (Kamran, Neda, Arman, Darya, Bahram, Sara, Mina). Applied to the HDPE plant's users and the setup seeder.
- **No money for operators:** operators see quantities, not valuation pages, value columns, value exports or the Reports link.
- **Production accountant does not edit stops:** coding, split, merge and bulk code by operators, production engineers, reliability engineers and manager approvers; the production accountant reconciles but has read-only coding.
- **Downtime summary follows every filter** (schedule class included); the scope line names the active filters.
- **Names without "training":** unit and product names drop "training" (codes unchanged).
- **Periods with no approved plan:** shown as "No approved plan covers this period" with a link to Plans; excluded from work lists and month-close prerequisites until planned.
- **Closed records are frozen:** a closed investigation and a decided restatement posting are read-only (server + UI); data repairs are recorded in the record's history.

## 2026-10-19 — QA audit: master data, finance and integration fixes

From `docs/qa/QA_MASTERDATA.md` (M1–M8 and lows).

- **Built-in defaults:** a version seeded at the epoch sentinel (≤ 1970-01-02 UTC) reads "Built-in default (from the start)" everywhere (`ux-kit/format/built-in-default.ts`, `ux-kit/effective-from.tsx`), never "1970-01-01 03:30". Server seed notes are plain words ("Built-in default; change it by recording a new version."); notes already stored with the old internal text ("Seeded on first use …", "Seeded by migration …") are mapped on display — no data migration. The stop-engine "Change notes" field no longer pre-fills a seed note.
- **People, not "Another user":** finance versions show the author's and the approver's name ("(you)" after the viewer's own, "System" for a seeded default); the policy page trail is "Finance › Valuation policy › Version n".
- **Unit state coverage before the first mapping:** `GET /v2/unit-state-mappings/coverage` counts from the first mapping version in the window and returns it as `coverageStartsAt` (additive; null when the whole window is covered); the page says "Coverage starts at …, when the first state mapping came into force". The time before the first mapping is not unknown time.
- **Durations in readiness text:** server detail text uses the screen duration rule ("17 h 03 min", `domain/time/duration-text.ts`).
- **Counters show no time base:** a counter (totalizer) shows its unit alone ("t"); only a rate shows "per hour".
- **Readiness window = whole plant days:** "From" / "To (inclusive)" like the coverage pickers, sent as `[start of first day, start of the day after the last)`; the default is the last whole plant day.
- **Equipment setup steps open read-only:** a master-data administrator opens master-data steps without their write capability; the page decides what can be saved. "SuperAdmin can perform every role personally" is shown to SuperAdmin only.
- **New valuation policy version:** needs `canMaintainValuation` as a route gate; a fresh version starts from the default basis of the approved version in force.
- **Small wording:** FX rates with thousands separators and every significant digit; historian sample values with the signal unit and 2 decimals; MQTT message/persist facts and counters are labelled "since the worker started"; one empty state for every code library; "(pending)" only when the name does not already say it; "Save changes" disabled until the record is edited; the new-code dialog checks the code pattern and duplicates inline.
- **Code tree a11y:** nested sub-code lists are plain `<ul>` (no `role="group"`, which removed list semantics and caused axe `listitem`).

## 2026-10-19 — QA audit: naming, units and shell fixes

From `docs/qa/QA_SHELL.md`, `QA_MASTERDATA.md` M9 / L23 and `QA_SUMMARY.md` priority 3.

- **One page name:** the menu name (`NAVIGATION_ITEMS[].name`, read with `pageNameOf(href)`) is also the page h1 / tab title, the breadcrumb step and the No-access title, in sentence case per the glossary: "Pending approvals", "Plans, profiles & calendars", "Month close", "Measurement uncertainty", "Bad actors", "Knowledge base", "Source status rules", "Source priority", "Enhanced historian", "Equipment setup guide", "Master data", "User management", "Admin panel", "Audit log", sections "Downtime analysis" / "Production loss analysis (PLA)". Product names keep their capitals: "Event Hub" (glossary module name) and "RCA Analysis" (glossary; the RCA list h1 now says "RCA Analysis" instead of "Root cause analysis"); acronyms (PLA, OEE, MTBF/MTTR) and "Pareto" too. `src/features/auth/page-names.test.ts` holds every menu name to sentence case and to its page's h1.
- **Downtime "Reliability" page** is "Reliability (MTBF/MTTR)" (menu, breadcrumb, h1 suffix), so it no longer reads like the Reliability section below it.
- **Breadcrumbs:** consecutive steps with the same address or the same words in another case are one step ("Admin panel › Edit Marun"). A step or back link whose route gate the user does not hold is plain text / left out (`CrumbReachFromRoutes` judges by the route gates, so Sara's "Connectivity" on Source priority is no longer a link).
- **Figure and unit:** always a space between a figure and its unit or currency ("28.00 t", "4,200.00 USD"): `ux-kit/format/units.ts` (`withUnit`, `formatQuantity`); `<Quantity>` keeps the space in the figure's own text node and `formatCurrencyAmount` uses `withUnit`. There is no ux-kit barrel file, so the helpers are imported from `ux-kit/format/*`.
- **Counts with nouns:** `plural(count, one, many, formatCount?)` (`ux-kit/format/plural.ts`, plant digits via `useNumberFormat().number`) and `plural()` on the server (`common/helpers/plural.ts`) replace every user-facing "(s)" (bell reminder "1 stop waiting for a reason", coding gate, imports, RCA tools, integration stages, error messages) and "1 groups". Log lines and the "(s)" seconds unit stay as they are.
- **Sign-in:** the field is "Email", the h1 is "Sign in"; the brand stays above it as plain text. **404:** h1 "Page not found", 404 as a visual tag.
- **Approvals:** `/approvals/<unknown kind>/<id>` is checked against the inbox kinds before any request and shows "Approval not found" (h1 + tab title) with "Back to Pending approvals". The Workflow filter lives in `?kind=`. States and roles read in sentence case ("Awaiting posting", "Finance approver"). A shared 404 (`DataState` with `back`) offers "Back to <list>" instead of Retry / "Reload the page" (tasks, audit entry, organization / plant edit).
- **Audit log:** export jobs are named by report and format ("Created export · Period summary CSV"; the job id is the record id). Summaries never show the raw verb or "unknown" ("CREATE Exports "unknown"" now reads "Created export"; older rows too, at read time). Sign-in/out rows (`USER`) read "User session", so "Audit entries by record type" no longer lists "User" twice, and rows that read the same are merged. POSTs whose last path segment is `preview`, `validate` or `dry-run` are not audited (next to the downtime track's `@SkipAuditOnPreview`).
- **Header ≤ 1023 px:** the brand name is hidden below `lg`, so the plant scope gets the space first.
- **Admin panel:** the description names only the visible tabs; branding says "color" (US spelling).
- **Dashboard:** cards no longer repeat their section title ("Downtime totals" under Downtime, "Investigations and actions" under Root cause analysis).

## 2026-10-19 — QA audit: RCA fixes

From `docs/qa/QA_RCA.md` (H1, M1–M5 and lows), under the owner decision "Closed records are frozen".

- **Closed investigation = read-only (H1):** every write on a `Closed` case answers **409 CASE_CLOSED** (`rca-case/case-closed.ts`): case PATCH and DELETE, logic-tree reset, the six analysis-tool PUTs, evidence-file uploads, action create / PATCH / verify / baseline correction propose and decide, cause create / PATCH / DELETE, links (already), Task Management tasks created or relinked to the case (`linkType RCA`), and the retired v1 update. A cause write on a closed case now gets 409 CASE_CLOSED instead of 422 CAUSES_LOCKED (Closure Review keeps 422 CAUSES_LOCKED). Reopen (`POST /v2/rcas/{id}/transitions`, independent approver) is the only path. *Kept:* Closure Review stays editable except causes and team (unchanged); the flow-back of a linked task's completion onto its action (`taskCompletedAt`, audited) still runs — it records a Task Management fact, not an edit of the investigation; the hourly action-verification sweep now skips the actions of a closed investigation (they are evaluated once it is reopened).
  - *UI:* the workspace is read-only when the case is closed (`readOnly = !canWriteRca || closed`, `canWrite` kept for the workflow so Reopen stays available); writers see "Closed investigation — reopen to change" under the tabs; action rows (status, Edit, Verify now, baseline correction, add action, create task) follow the same rule.
- **Data repairs in the case History (M1):** a repair migration that writes `rcas` appends one History entry per changed investigation with `recordCaseRepair` (`src/database/case-repair-history.ts`): actor System (`service` `data_repair`), action `rca_case.repaired` ("Data repair"), the repair's reason in words, the changed fields, timed at the repair's write; `removeCaseRepair` is its `down` (a scoped retention-cutoff delete, since `audit_log` is append-only). Migration `RecordClosedRecommendationRepairHistory1794222810000` records the 2026-10-18 18:47Z repair (`CloseRecommendationsOfClosedCases`) on each closed case whose row version is newer than its last case-row History entry and that holds a final recommendation. Rule for future repairs: use the helper in the same migration.
- **"Verified" badge (M2):** an evaluated action's badge is its verdict ("Effective", "Not effective", "Not enough evidence yet"), "Verdict recorded" when none is stored; the word "Verified" is no longer used for verification status.
- **One valuation basis on Links & outcome (M3):** the outcome's approved historical loss is valued in the view frozen in the case's first evidence package (event or loss-line link), and its basis says so ("… valued in the economic impact view (the basis frozen in the linked evidence package)"); the evidence package's value line reads "Value: Economic impact (at link time)". No "policy default" claim and no internal references ("SME V8", "SME CA9") in user text; the basis is a capitalised sentence.
- **Exports (M4):** the JSON adds every analysis tool (5-Why, fishbone, FMEA, fault tree, barrier, change; without record bookkeeping) and the History (as on the History page, read page by page at export time); new **Actions (CSV)**; the evidence CSV uses display labels and adds observed time (plant and UTC), asset and file count. Copy link awaits the clipboard and toasts success or the failure (with the link to copy by hand).
- **Evidence-collection items are "Evidence requests", not Tasks (M5):** they stay inside the evidence item (no Task Management task is created for them — they have no lifecycle, assignee notification or My work entry of their own); screens and exports call them "Evidence request" / "Request" / "Requested from" / "Request due" / "Request status". "Task" stays reserved for Task Management items (glossary). Making them real tasks is a later product decision if collectors need them in My work.
- **Words (lows):** a linked downtime event is a "Stop" (case header, Links, timeline "Stop · Gearbox failure", Recommended queue); buttons say investigation ("Save investigation", "Start investigation", "Create or link investigation", "Similar past investigations"); case statuses in sentence case ("In progress", "Closure review"), the legacy "Published" status is not offered as a list filter; a finished recommendation, action or evidence request reads "Done" with the success tone; failure-mode keys show the dictionary name (else humanized); RCA policy method keys show names ("5-Why or Fishbone or Logic tree"), the read-only summary states the minimum state-data coverage, the seeded version 1 reads "Built-in default (from the start)", and an out-of-range percent shows an inline error; History hides bookkeeping fields (ID, RCA ID, Tenant ID, investigators, target id, created by) and names cause codes ("CODE — name"); an event whose investigation is completed no longer offers "Create or link investigation".
- **Action thresholds name their policy (L11):** new baselines freeze `criteria.policyVersion` (additive; server `computeBaseline`), shown as "Thresholds frozen from RCA policy vN when the verification was planned"; older baselines say "from the RCA policy in force when the verification was planned".
- **New investigation form (L12 / L15):** required marker on Title, "Enter a title to create the investigation." next to the disabled button, an unsaved-changes prompt on Cancel / links / back; non-writers on `/rca/new` get the "New investigation" heading and tab title with the refusal. Definition edits left on another tab are announced there ("Unsaved changes to the definition are kept as a draft for this session") instead of a prompt, keeping the session-draft design.

## 2026-10-19 — QA audit: downtime and Event Hub fixes

From `docs/qa/QA_DOWNTIME.md` (F1–F6, lows) under the owner decisions "Production accountant does not edit stops" and "Downtime summary follows every filter". No calculation changed.

- **Event writers (Q1 / Q3):** one list, `EVENT_WRITERS` = operator, production engineer, reliability engineer, manager approver (`v2/production-events/event-write-roles.ts`; SuperAdmin / OrgAdmin satisfy it, legacy roles through their D06 role). `@Roles(...EVENT_WRITERS)` on create, PATCH, merge, bulk code (preview included), split, close, retract, review-item resolve and the repeat-stop "new failure" mark / unmark; `STOP_DECISION_ROLES` (duplicate-stop decisions and the inbox `stop-merge` item) **is** `EVENT_WRITERS`, so who sees the item is who decides it. Everyone else (production accountant, viewer, and the approver / admin roles the decision does not name: quality, metrology, finance, integration, master data) gets 403 FORBIDDEN_ROLE. UI: `canWriteEvents` mirrors the list (`server-role-sets.ts`, pinned by `server-role-sets.test.ts`); `/events/new` and `/events/:id/edit` are gated by it; a writer elsewhere who reads events sees "Events: read-only for your role". Tasks keep their old rule (any non-viewer) under a new `canWriteTasks`. The v1 `/api/production-events` controller is unchanged (legacy, write-frozen).
- **Downtime summary follows the schedule class (Q2 / F2):** `GET /reports/downtime-bundle` takes an optional `scheduleClass` (additive; response adds `scheduleClass`). With it, total downtime and stops are the sums of the stored breakdown cells of that class (the same cells as the class trend, so tiles and trend agree), the class's own period KPI (planned / unplanned / external / unclassed time) stays as stored, and every other figure (availability, coded share, means, carry-in, …) is returned without a value, reason `not_by_schedule_class` ("Not split by schedule class"). The summary tiles say "Figures for planned stops only…". Product / shift / crew already narrowed the summary server-side; the scope line now names every active filter (products, shifts, crews, schedule class, micro-stops, approved only). `/reports/downtime` keeps its class-independent summary.
- **Previous-period comparison (F1):** a page that shows only the summary still asks the bundle for one view (the trend series it would show), because the API requires 1–12 views; the earlier window uses the same bundle request, so both sides follow the same filters. A contract test reads the server DTO (view grammar, declared parameters).
- **Event form (F3):** an end time at or before the start is refused at the End field while typing and on Save ("The end must be after the start"); the server stays the authority (422 END_BEFORE_START lands on the same field). The impacted-periods preview uses start + duration in duration mode and is not queried for a reversed range, so the server's refusal no longer appears under "Impacted periods".
- **Words on the event page (F4):** failure-dictionary fields show the dictionary name ("Trip alarm"; free text as typed), failure severity its label; "APM alert type" and "Safe operating limit type" instead of a second "Event type"; History notes in words ("Created", "Coding verified", "Failure detail validated", "Investigation linked / closed", "Bulk code: …"); "Investigation state" / "Investigation" filter (glossary). The state-chip values from `ux-kit` (`No RCA`, `RCA completed`) and the RCA trigger method names are left to the label / RCA tracks.
- **Bulk code (F5):** a preview (`preview: true`) is not written to the audit log (`@SkipAuditOnPreview`, body flag only on marked routes). The preview adds `changedCount` and per-item `changed` (additive; `count` stays the eligible count); the dialog counts real changes ("0 events change · 2 already coded this way"), marks unchanged rows, lists the recalculated windows one per line and disables "Apply" with a reason when nothing changes.
- **Split slider (F6):** the drag handle is named "Split at" and reads the chosen plant time (`aria-valuetext`); built from the Radix primitive because the generated `ui/slider` cannot name its thumb.
- **Lows:** an event opened from an analysis page keeps that page (with its filters) as the breadcrumb parent; the edit page's middle crumb is the event's title; event and edit pages open at the top; the listed Pareto bar is in the URL (`drill=`), so Back from a stop restores the list (a filter change closes it); a one-day window of chosen bounds names its day ("Custom range · 2026-10-17 06:00–06:00"); the shift list stops at the current shift; "Apply range" is disabled while the typed range is reversed or incomplete; the event CSV keeps its byte order mark when saved and appends `start_plant` / `end_plant` (ISO with offset, plant clock) and `*_label` columns (screen words; raw columns unchanged); "SME D4 / D12" and "D13 default: FailureWO …" are plain sentences now.

## 2026-10-19 — QA audit: planning, period accounting and PLA analytics fixes

From `docs/qa/QA_PLANNING.md` (P-0 … P-14, lows) and `docs/qa/QA_PLA_ANALYTICS.md` (M1–M6, lows) under the owner decisions "Periods with no approved plan" and "Closed records are frozen". No calculation changed; new response fields are additive.

- **Decided restatement posting is final (closed records are frozen):** `POST /v2/production-periods/:id/revisions/:revision/posting` decides once; a second decision answers **409 POSTING_DECIDED** and the row is never overwritten (`restatement_postings.row_version` stays 1, audit unchanged). The correction path is a new restatement: propose a correction of the period; its new approved revision gets its own posting decision. The restatement view adds `posting.decidedByName`; the panel shows "posted on … as … · decided by <name> on <plant time>" and, for finance, "The posting decision is final…" instead of the form. Saving a posting asks for confirmation ("The decision is final"). Treatment words stay sentence-case fragments ("as a correction").
- **Value-only restatements show their value (P-2):** the restatement view adds `values` (`oldView`, `newView`, `viewChanged`, per currency `old` / `new` / `delta`, server-computed from each revision's frozen valuation in its own default view); the panel's first row is "Loss value (Economic impact → Contribution margin)" in the display currency (Marun shift A: 5,040.00 → 5,250.00 USD, +210.00). The approval inbox's restatement / revision change set adds the same "Loss value (default valuation view)" money rows (one per currency, grouped).
- **Change proposal before / after (P-1):** curated figures with units in a before | after table, never a field dump: **Reconciliation** (with the review decisions: Target, Actual (good), Gap, Attributed losses, Tolerance adjustment, Unaccounted) kept apart from **Plan ledger (calculated)** and **Capacity ledger (calculated)** (target / capacity, actual, availability, performance, quality loss, Unaccounted before review decisions). Ids, hashes, flags and internal fields (unadjusted residual, exclusion, over-accounted, uom) are not shown; several slices are named "slice n"; values are the server's, never summed. The proposal view adds `valueAfter` (`revision`, `lifecycle`, default-view `values`) for an accepted proposal — the value of the revision the acceptance opened, as it stands — so "Loss value after (revision 3, approved, Contribution margin) 5,250.00 USD" replaces "Calculated when the proposal is accepted"; a dismissed proposal says the approved value stays. One revision state is read per accepted proposal of the page (a period has few).
- **Periods with no approved plan (P-0):** a period is plan-covered when its slices were resolved against a plan version or an approved accounting-baseline plan version overlaps it (`close/plan-coverage-sql.ts`). Uncovered periods are left out of My work (reconciliation queue), SLA reminders and escalation notifications, and every month-close prerequisite (periods, days, scheduled shifts without a period); month close lists them as information (`prerequisites.uncoveredDayCount` / `uncoveredDays`: unit, date, number of shifts) with "N production days have no approved plan. Their periods are not counted above…" and a link to Plans (`/planning?tab=plans`, for users who can open analysis). Pending periods carry `planId` and link to their page; a queue or close row without its plan says "No approved plan covers this period · Open Plans". The reconciliation status report (PLA) is unchanged: it reports every period.
- **Month close finance steps (P-7):** a finance approver sees "Finance sign-off…" and "Lock month…" from the start, disabled with the reason until reachable ("Plant management closes the quantities first…", "The materiality review comes first.", "Finance signs off the month first…").
- **Planning forms, period page, URL state and gates (P-3, P-4, P-5, lows):** plan, plan version, profile, disposition, adjustment and calendar forms mark required fields and ask before discarding unsaved edits. A new version of an approved plan requires a change reason and a confirmation and keeps the plan's unit. The period title names unit, plant-format date and shift; the "Not coded" chip appears only on lines with a cause stop; an operator sees no Review & close link and no breadcrumb trail to planning pages. Disabled Record run / Verify this shift's stops / Import / Run preview say why. The grid's "Figures of" (`view=approved`) and the planning unit picker (`unitId=`, the name existing deep links use) live in the URL; New profile / New calendar routes are gated by `canWriteProfiles` / `canWriteCalendars`.
- **PLA production-day window (M1):** a window whose bounds fall on the same plant time other than midnight is a run of production days named by the date each starts on: "Chosen dates · 2026-10-17 (production day 06:00–06:00)" (date in the plant format). Choosing dates keeps that start time; PLA and Reports date inputs are typed in the plant date format. One label for a window chosen by dates: "Chosen dates".
- **Loss events "Lost quantity (attributed)" (M2):** the loss attributed to the event by the period loss lines (approved, else draft) — the same lines as the loss tree (`GET /v2/reports/loss-tree?groupBy=event`, additive; groups add `eventLines`) — never the event's own recorded quantity.
- **Quality drill-down (M3) and "Not coded":** quality losses come from lot dispositions, so their bars / rows open the periods' Actuals & quality (`?periods=quality` → "Open dispositions"); reason rows made only of manual lines have no event link and say why. The loss tree by reason keeps "Quality (dispositions)" (`quality_dispositions`) apart from "Not coded"; the asset grouping of repeat causes says "No asset recorded".
- **Reports coverage:** the share of the window's periods whose figures are shown — under "Approved revisions only" approved periods ("43 % of periods approved"), otherwise calculated periods ("86 % of periods calculated", as on the Dashboard). With approved-only, missing periods are reported as incomplete, not "Provisional"; captions follow the toggle; disabled exports give their reason in words.
- **Lows (PLA / Bad actors):** one display-currency toggle per screen (the header one); a figure and its unit or currency are always separated by a space ("28.00 t", "4,200.00 USD", `ux-kit` `Quantity` / `withUnit`); "OEE" keeps its capitals in headings; counts are pluralised ("1 group"); period labels read "2026-10-17 C · 22:00"; cost views with no input say "Not valued (<reason>)" instead of "Provisional"; the trends tooltip uses the popover tokens; the Bad actors failure mode shows the dictionary name and its table is a focusable, labelled scroll region; the Dashboard "All repeat causes" link uses `?breakdown=repeat-causes`.
- **Exports:** the CSV keeps every existing column in place and appends plant-time columns (`plant_start`, `plant_end`, `approved_at_plant`: ISO 8601 with the plant offset, Gregorian) after the UTC ones; the header adds `requested_by_name` and `plant_timezone`; loss lines append `line_source_label`, `kind_label`, `state_label`, `reason_code`, `reason_name` (`reason` stays the editor's comment). The PDF shows sources, kinds and states in words, has a Reason column ("CODE · Name", or the comment), says "Unaccounted" instead of "Residual" and names the requester. The requester's name, plant time zone and reason-code names are fixed on the job at request time (`export_jobs.labels`, migration `ExportJobLabels`), so a re-render reproduces the bytes; older jobs print these cells empty. Every CSV starts with a UTF-8 BOM.


## 2026-10-19 — QA audit: guide roles, no money for operators, approvals tile (engineering under the owner's QA audit decisions)

- **Legacy role = sign-in / display compatibility only (supersedes the D06 mapping as a standing grant).** The D06 legacy → scope role mapping (Analyst / Engineer → production engineer, TenantAdmin / Manager → manager approver, Operator → operator) is now a **fallback**: it applies in a plant only while the user holds no own grant there (`legacyRolesApplying`, server `request-context.factory.ts` and every consumer: unit roles, plant roles, approval scope, the inbox's materiality scope, delegations, notification role holders; frontend `effective-roles.ts`). With any own grant the plant roles are exactly the grants, plus the legacy role name itself (still used by `TenantAdmin` rules) and the global SuperAdmin / OrgAdmin. Delegated grants do not switch the fallback off.
- **Mirror is revocable.** The home-plant grant that mirrors the legacy role keeps its `home_role` lock only while it is the user's only plant-wide role in the home plant; with another plant-wide role it is an ordinary grant (Users › user › Access: Remove). A user can therefore have a legacy role that maps to no plant role.
- **No behaviour change on deploy.** Migration `1794222800000-LegacyRoleMirrorBackfill` inserts the mirror grant for any user who holds another grant in the home plant but lacks the mirror (deterministic ids; `down` deletes exactly those), so effective roles stay as they were until a mirror is removed on purpose.
- **Marun personas.** The setup seeder gives each person exactly the guide role (`step-people.ts` revokes every other plant-wide grant after granting the guide role); `scripts/marun-train/apply-guide-roles.ts` does the same on the existing plant through the API (idempotent, `--dry-run`). Live effect when the coordinator runs it: the production engineer mirror is removed from Kamran, Neda, Arman, Darya, Bahram, Sara, Mina and Farid; Shirin and Leila keep production engineer, Omid operator, Reza / Maryam manager approver.
- **Plant roles on screen.** The header badge shows the plant roles ("Finance approver", "+n" with the full list as tooltip), not "Analyst"; the users list column "Plant roles" reads `plantRoles` (additive on `UserV2View`: grants in the acting plant, else the D06 fallback in the home plant); the Permissions tab lists the menu pages the user reaches (`capabilitiesOf` + `NAVIGATION_ITEMS`), no longer a per-legacy-role table.
- **No money for operators:** new capability `canViewMoney` (every role but the operations-only operator roles). Gated: Loss valuation menu + route, the PLA header's Reports link and period-summary export, Top opportunities and the value metrics of the loss tree (a money metric in a link falls back to quantity), the USD / IRR toggle, the value of an event's attributed losses and of an investigation's approved impact. Reports / Finance / Bad actors were already closed to operators. Server responses are unchanged (UI rule; a server-side value redaction for operators is the follow-up if required).
- **Pending approvals tile** for every approver role (manager approver, production accountant, quality / metrology / finance approver, TenantAdmin / Manager, SuperAdmin / OrgAdmin), counting only what the caller can decide (`decidable=true`).


## 2026-10-19 — QA re-test decisions (standard practice)

- **Money is withheld on the server for operators:** every API response that carries valuation figures omits them for callers without the money capability (operator), and every money view/toggle/export/tab uses the same capability — not page-by-page hiding.
- **Unknown time is never available time:** downtime/OEE KPIs over windows with missing state data exclude the unknown time from scheduled/available time and show data coverage; days with no approved plan use the shared wording "No approved plan covers this period".
- **Production-day boundaries:** every date-only selection (Reports, readiness, analysis windows) maps to the plant's operational day (06:00–06:00 plant time), never to midnight or the current clock time.
- **Reliability engineers read the failure dictionary** (they validate failure detail); writing stays with master-data roles.
- **Stale work reminders:** when a user loses the role that made them a recipient, their open reminders for that work resolve.

## 2026-10-19 — QA re-test: RCA fixes, round 3 (engineering default under the QA re-test decisions)

From the "Re-test 19 Oct" section of `docs/qa/QA_RCA.md` (M3 partial, M7, L1, L6/N2, L8, L10, L11, L16, N3, N4). Money gating (N1) is handled under the money rule, not here.

- **One value basis for an approved figure (M3):** the investigation's approved loss (case header, Links & outcome) and new evidence packages are valued in the view frozen in the approval snapshots of the periods that carry the loss (`approval_snapshots.content.reviewed.valuation.defaultView`, latest approved revision per period) — the view the period itself shows; basis "… valued in the <view> view (the view frozen when the periods were approved)". When no approved period froze a view, or the periods froze different ones, the policy's current default view applies (named without a source). Supersedes the 19 Oct "basis frozen in the linked evidence package" rule for the outcome. Frozen packages keep their figures; their stored basis is shown without "(the policy default, SME V8)" (`publicEvidence` on read; nothing rewritten). One label everywhere: `valueBasisLabel` in `ux-kit/labels/value-basis.ts` ("Value basis: Contribution margin", from `DEFAULT_BASIS_LABELS`, note in brackets such as "at link time") on the evidence package, the outcome and Bad actors — the Event Hub attributed-losses card already prints the same words. *Open for the stop page and Bad actors:* they still value in the current policy default (their services belong to the downtime / reports areas); with the Marun data both match the snapshot view.
- **List Trigger column (L1):** "Stop 2026-10-17 08:00" (no trigger: "No linked stop"); the Recommended tab text says "Stops where an RCA trigger fired".
- **History (L8):** a case or child write whose list field (evidence, recommendations) changed records `detail.itemChanges` — item titles (≤ 120 chars, ≤ 20 items) with added / removed / edited and the edited field names, never ids or values; the History shows "Changed: Evidence: “Oil sample” edited (request status, verified)". Entries written before this say "Evidence (which item changed was not recorded)" (audit rows are append-only). `GET /v2/rcas/{id}/history` adds `totalCount` (additive), so the pager reads "1–20 of 43 · Page 1 of 3" from the first page.
- **Page name (L10):** the RCA list h1 and the case / new-investigation back links read the menu name through `pageNameOf('/rca')`; the name itself ("RCA Analysis" vs "RCA analysis") stays with the navigation owner.
- **Thresholds name the policy (L11):** action views add `baselinePolicyVersion` (additive): the version frozen with the criteria, else — on list reads — the `rca_policy` version (unit, else tenant-wide) in force at the baseline's `computedAt` (read only, never seeds); the UI shows "Thresholds frozen from RCA policy v1 …".
- **Residual risk note (M7):** under a case's residual risk, when an action's frozen run-hour minimum differs from the current policy's, the Overview adds "The actions' thresholds were frozen from RCA policy v1 (at least 720 run hours after an action). The current policy v2 asks for at least 600 run hours; it applies to new baselines." The text typed on the case is not changed.
- **Read-only event diagram (L6 / N2):** on a read-only (closed) case the diagram has no "Add connection / drag" hint ("No connections recorded.") and no interactivity toggle; zoom / fit and the React Flow attribution stay.
- **My work → RCA actions status filter (L16):** `MyRcaActionsList` takes `statusFilter` (To do / Done / All, starting at `status`; not with `overdue`); the My work page (owned by the shell area) is to pass `statusFilter` — not wired here.
- **Bad actors (N3):** no "(CA8)" in the subtitle; the basis is shown as a capitalised sentence (`basisSentence`).
- **Repair reason (N4):** the History shows recorded data repairs in its own words by repair name (`rca-case/repair-reasons.ts`): "Recommendations completed after the investigation closed: each recommendation was set to Done or Cancelled from the statuses of its actions (…)"; other repairs drop a leading "Data repair:". The stored audit text and the applied migration are unchanged; future repairs write their reason without the prefix and with display status words.

## 2026-10-19 — QA re-test: downtime and Event Hub fixes (round 3, `docs/qa/QA_DOWNTIME.md` "Re-test 19 Oct")

Under the re-test decisions "Unknown time is never available time" and "Production-day boundaries". Money gating (N1, N3) is the money track's `canViewMoney` / server redaction and is not duplicated here.

- **Unknown time rule (N5), as implemented.** A period's unknown state seconds are the `unknownSeconds` the state engine already stores in its `state_coverage` KPI row (SME D2: no data, stale, bad quality, unmapped). Over a report window (`/reports/downtime`, `/reports/downtime-bundle` summary, `/reports/reliability` units) they leave the time terms per period, clamped at 0: scheduled and controllable availability = (run time − U) ÷ (scheduled − U), i.e. 1 − downtime ÷ known scheduled time (a stop recorded inside unknown time stays a stop, so the rule never raises availability); onstream factor = (calendar − downtime − U) ÷ (calendar − U); MTBF = (exposure − U) ÷ failures. A window with no known time has no value, reason `no_known_time` ("No state data covers this window"), never 0 %. Stored per-period KPI rows and the KPI engine are unchanged (no engine version bump, no recalculation): a period with fully known state (U = 0) and a unit without a state mapping (no row) keep their figures, so the 17 Oct production day (state published 05:30 → 06:00 next day, U = 0) stays 93.75 % / MTBF 22 h 30 min, and a 7-day window that includes 18–19 Oct (no state data) now gives the same 93.75 % instead of 97.3 %. Stop time, counts, coded share and the breakdowns never change. OEE (`/reports/oee`) is not adjusted: its availability is the OEE policy's R ÷ S and counts only periods with an OEE (A × P × Q must stay equal to OEE); recorded as a follow-up if the owner wants it there too.
- **Data coverage (N5).** The three responses add `dataCoverage` (additive; response interfaces are not in the OpenAPI document): periods with state data, scheduled / known / unknown seconds, `ratio` = Σ known ÷ Σ scheduled, and `periodsWithoutPlan` (shown periods no approved plan covers, `planCoveredSql`). The downtime summary and the Reliability card say "Data covers X % of the window. Time without state data is not counted as scheduled or available time, nor in MTBF." and "N periods in this window: No approved plan covers this period · Open Plans" (the shared `NoPlanLink`). A period with no plan and no accounting in Impacted periods (New event, event Periods tab) reads the same instead of "Not calculated".
- **Production-day date fields (L20).** The downtime "Period dates" pick production days at the plant's operational day start (`useOperationalDayStart`, 06:00): one day picked = 06:00–06:00, and that window reads From 17 Oct, To (inclusive) 17 Oct. The shared date-range input is unchanged; the downtime page maps its plant midnights to day-start bounds (`production-day-range.ts`).
- **Words.** Investigation state chips and filter options come from ux-kit `EVENT_RCA_STATE_LABELS`: "No investigation", "Investigation recommended", "Investigation linked", "Investigation completed" (chips and recommendation badges read them; the RCA trigger setting reads "Recommend an investigation"); the similar-investigation score reads "Record quality 80 / 100"; a system revision of a named service reads "Data repair by the system"; periods read "2026-10-17 · Shift A" in the Periods tab and the losses card, and an approved period opens `?view=approved`; the downtime unit chip reads "Unit: hours and minutes"; the Code dialog of an ended stop gives its span; counts use the plural helper (trend summary, bulk batches, incident stops); trend legend and summary use the class labels; each downtime page has its own subtitle.
- **Display.** Lost value shows the header display currency's table only (USD default), every currency when the scope has no amount in it (N2). Filter buttons name one or two chosen values ("Shifts: A"), else the count. The Evidence tab leaves empty source fields out and counts them (raw payload unchanged). The Repair tab drops the "Open the stop" column (same page). The window selects show the current preset once, selected. The controllable view is in the URL (`controllable=1`). The RCA trigger page has no "New version" section for roles that cannot change it. The downtime export states its disabled reason on screen (`aria-describedby`). Chart tooltips use the popover colours (axe contrast) and axis durations do not wrap ("1 h 15 min").

## 2026-10-19 — QA re-test round 3: planning, period accounting, PLA analytics and Reports (engineering under the owner's re-test decisions "Production-day boundaries" and "Periods with no approved plan")

From the "Re-test 19 Oct" sections of `docs/qa/QA_PLANNING.md`, `docs/qa/QA_PLA_ANALYTICS.md` and the Reports items of `docs/qa/QA_SHELL.md`. No calculation changed; every new response field is additive.

- **Reports and PLA windows on production days (Reports N1 / N4, PLA shift C / N6 / N8):** date-only picks map to the plant production day (the default site's operational day start, 06:00–06:00 plant time; calendar days when no site exists), never midnight or the current clock. On Reports a rolling window covers whole production days, the last n that ended by its end ("Last 7 days" at 19 Oct 15:04 = 12 Oct 06:00 → 19 Oct 06:00). PLA rolling windows stay live and show their real bounds ("Last 7 days · 2026-10-12 15:04 – 2026-10-19 15:04"); a window inside one production day reads "Production day 2026-10-17 · 22:00–06:00". The URL carries machine values (`window=7d`, `window=dates`); older label values still read. A Gregorian date field typed in another format says "Use YYYY-MM-DD, e.g. 2026-10-17". Links from PLA into Downtime / Event Hub still carry the label values those pages read (follow-up with the downtime owners).
- **Reports provisional wording:** under "Approved revisions only" no card says "Provisional" when periods are only missing; the coverage note says the scope is incomplete (one rule, `model/provisional.ts`, header and cards). The Revisions select reads "Approved revisions only" / "Include open drafts (provisional figures)". `GET /v2/reports/oee` adds `groups[].noApprovedPlan` (date, shift, week, month, period, unit and crew groups; `planCoveredSql`); such a row reads "No approved plan covers this period · Open Plans" once, with "—" in its figures.
- **Lines with no event (PLA M3 re-test):** a drill-down whose loss has no event lists the lines themselves. `GET /v2/reports/lines-without-event` (new, read-only) returns the loss-tree lines with no cause event, 50 largest plus a `total`, with reason code, the editor's reason and comment, evidence (SME R11) and the period link; Loss events shows them under the event list. Quantities are canonical decimals ("150").
- **Bad actors:** the "not approved yet" count leaves out periods no approved plan covers; they are named apart (`uncoveredPeriods`, "No approved plan covers these periods"). The window shows inclusive plant dates; the basis is sentence case without rule codes; an asset row opens its unplanned stops of the window in the Event Hub. The review month follows the month-close month-key rule: a key of the other calendar (`1405-07` on a Gregorian plant) is **422 MONTH_KEY_AMBIGUOUS** instead of being read as Gregorian year 1405 (behaviour change on a previously accepted, wrong input).
- **Approved loss keeps the view it was approved in (Bad actors and the stop page):** like the investigation (`rca-case/approved-snapshot-view.ts`), the Bad actors value column and a stop's Attributed losses are valued in the view frozen in the approval snapshots of the periods carrying the loss; the policy's current default view only when no approved period froze one or they froze different ones. Both responses add where the view comes from (`viewSource` / `valuationViewSource`: `approved_snapshot` | `policy_default`); both screens use ux-kit `valueBasisLabel`.
- **Exports:** a stop-caused line without a reason code reads "Not coded" (CSV `reason_name`, PDF Reason). The CSV already carried `reason_code` / `reason_name` (MECH-GEARBOX / Gearbox failure) in its appended columns; `reason` stays the editor's comment. PDF cells wrap inside their row and the row grows to its tallest cell (no overprint); the Reason column is wider.
